# B TECH syllabi

1812 documents (355 teaching schemes, 888 exam papers and answer keys filed with the syllabi). [`courses.jsonl`](courses.jsonl) has every course section found in them, with its text. Each branch file lists its documents; syllabus documents link to their courses (filed by subject), exam papers link to their subject in the question bank, and teaching schemes and other documents are given in full.
